([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-shift-tolerance-scoring) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-skill-authoring**: Writes or reviews a Mass Solutions skill so it passes the catalog validator on the first run. Use when "create a new skill", "review this SKILL.md" or "why does the validator reject my skill". Do NOT use for writing commit messages or pull requests (use mass-commit-message or mass-pr-description). ([maiconsouza89/agents-skills](https://github.com/maiconsouza89/agents-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mass-skill-authoring) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spec-tolerance-parameter-application**: Use when when you have a feature table from Orbitrap LC-MS containing m/z, retention time, and intensity columns, and you need to group individual mass features into putative metabolites that represent the same chemical entity across different ionization states and isotopic compositions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spec-tolerance-parameter-application) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectra-clustering-hyperdimensional-space**: Use when you have large-scale MS/MS spectra datasets (hundreds of thousands to millions of spectra) in MGF format that need to be grouped by similarity, and you have access to NVIDIA GPU hardware (GTX 1080Ti or GTX 3090). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectra-clustering-hyperdimensional-space) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectra-embedding-extraction**: Use when you have tandem mass spectra (MS/MS) in .msp format and need dense, chemically meaningful vector representations for library matching, similarity computation, or structural clustering. Apply this when comparing spectra across large reference databases (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectra-embedding-extraction) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectra-encoding-neural-network**: Use when you have a collection of MS/MS spectra (in mzML or MGF format) from a proteomics experiment and need to group or retrieve spectra derived from the same peptide without prior peptide identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectra-encoding-neural-network) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectra-tokenization-unified-vocabulary**: Use when when you have paired mass spectra and molecular structure data and need to train a unified model for structure elucidation. Use this skill at the data preparation stage before pretraining, when you want both modalities to share representational capacity rather than operate in isolation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectra-tokenization-unified-vocabulary) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-component-extraction**: Use when raw GC-MS data (netCDF or mzML format) contains overlapping chromatographic peaks with co-eluting ions that cannot be resolved by retention time alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-component-extraction) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-data-augmentation**: Use when when you have limited real GC-MS overlapped peak data but need thousands of labeled examples to train a deep learning model for mass spectral deconvolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-data-augmentation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-data-formatting**: Use when when you have raw mass spectral data in .mgf, .msp, .mzML, or .lbm2 file formats and need to search against a spectral library using entropy similarity or Flash Entropy Search. Also apply this skill before building spectral library indices or computing entropy-based compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-data-formatting) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-data-validation**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS spectral data in vendor formats or unvalidated .mgf files before feeding them into the specXplore importing pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-data-validation) · ★15 · 5 copies)
- **mass-spectral-feature-alignment**: Use when when you have separate LC-MS peak tables for unlabeled (C12) and labeled (C13) isotope tracer experiments and need to identify which features correspond to the same metabolite across the two labeling conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-feature-alignment) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-feature-annotation**: Use when you have m/z values from spatially-resolved mass spectrometry imaging (e.g., MALDI-MSI, DESI-MSI) and need to assign molecular formulae to thousands of features with higher precision than traditional LC-MS approaches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-feature-annotation) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-feature-grouping**: Use when you have untargeted metabolomics MS/MS spectra from multiple features and need to identify which features belong to the same molecular family or are related by biotransformation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-feature-grouping) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-fingerprint-generation**: Use when you have unaligned MS2 spectra from one or more samples (in formats like .mgf, .mzML, or .mzXML) and need to compare them in a retention-time-agnostic manner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-fingerprint-generation) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-library-benchmark-execution**: Use when you have access to the Flash Entropy Search implementation and want to validate that real-time spectral library querying achieves the reported timing and throughput metrics under the same library sizes, query parameters, and measurement methodology documented in the paper. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-library-benchmark-execution) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-m-z-alignment**: Use when after you have (1) identified putative labelled features with intensity and m/z measurements from LC/MS data (e.g., via basepeak_finder output in geoRge), (2) defined a list of expected ionization adducts (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-m-z-alignment) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-matrix-prediction**: Use when you have raw GC-MS data with overlapped peaks in a specific retention time region and need to resolve the individual pure mass spectra of all components present in that region. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-matrix-prediction) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-metadata-harmonization**: Use when you have multiple mass spectral libraries in different formats (NIST MSP + MOL folder, MoNA MSP, RIKEN MSP, SWGDRUG MSP) and need to merge them into a single, MS-DIAL-compatible MSP file with consistent SMILES assignments, Kovats retention indices (RI), and polarity annotations across all. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-metadata-harmonization) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-metadata-standardization**: Use when you have acquired EI or MS/MS spectral libraries from multiple public sources (NIST, RIKEN, MoNA, SWGDRUG, GNPS) with inconsistent metadata field layouts, missing or misplaced SMILES entries, undocumented retention indices, or mixed polarity modes, and you need to merge them into a single. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-metadata-standardization) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-missing-word-fraction-computation**: Use when when applying a pre-trained Spec2Vec Word2Vec model to new mass spectra (particularly those outside the model's training distribution), you need to assess whether peaks and neutral losses in query spectra have been seen during model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-missing-word-fraction-computation) · ★15 · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectral-network-annotation**: Use when you have a GNPS molecular network (classical or feature-based) and MS2LDA LDA experiment output (Mass2Motif assignments with probability and overlap scores) from the same experiment, and you want to annotate network nodes with structural motifs and chemical classes to infer molecular. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-network-annotation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-network-annotation**: Use when you have a GNPS-generated molecular network (graphml or JSON format) and corresponding MS2LDA experiment results or chemical class assignments, and you want to annotate network nodes with substructural motifs or chemical classes to facilitate structural interpretation and identify. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectral-network-annotation) · ★15)
- **mass-spectral-peak-annotation**: Use when you have centroided MS2 spectra (in mzML format from data-dependent acquisition) and a list of known or suspect PFAS diagnostic fragment masses, and you need to systematically flag which detected features contain fragments characteristic of PFAS compounds (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-peak-annotation) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-query-submission**: Use when you have one or more individual MS/MS spectra (in mzML, mzXML, or JSON format) and need to identify the compound(s) and their biological source by searching against a domain-specific spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-query-submission) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-relationship-matching**: Use when after peak detection and feature table generation when you have a collection of m/z, retention time, and intensity values and need to identify which features are related variants (isotopes, adducts, or fragments) of the same parent compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-relationship-matching) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectral-similarity-binning-and-stratification**: Use when you have predicted structural similarity scores (e.g., Tanimoto or Dice scores) for a large set of spectrum pairs and need to assess prediction accuracy across the full range of possible similarities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectral-similarity-binning-and-stratification) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectral-similarity-scoring-across-samples**: Use when after XCMS feature detection, grouping, and retention time correction when you have aligned features with consistent retention times and intensity patterns across samples. - **mass-spectrometer-simulator-configuration**: Use when when you have a list of chemical compounds (with m/z values, retention times, and intensities) and need to simulate their acquisition behavior under a specific ionization polarity and mass spectrometer configuration. - **mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation**: Use when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectra with measured precursor m/z values but the ionization adduct type is unknown or ambiguous—particularly in metabolomics workflows where multiple adducts co-occur or in de novo formula annotation without access to curated spectral databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation) · ★15)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-adduct-annotation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-adduct-assignment**: Use when when processing in-silico or experimental MS spectra records from databases with incomplete metadata, specifically when the adduct field is null or absent but the ionmode field (positive/negative polarity) is present. - **mass-spectrometry-adduct-nomenclature-and-formula-transformation**: Use when you have a neutral molecular formula (e.g., C3H8O2) and need to compute the adducted formula that will actually be observed in MS data; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-adduct-nomenclature-and-formula-transformation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-annotation-engine-customization**: Use when when you have baseline MS/MS peak annotations from a known compound but need to refine them using newly available structural information (e. - **mass-spectrometry-base-peak-identification**: Use when after PuInc_seeker has identified putative incorporations in XCMS-processed LC/MS data, when you have paired unlabeled and labeled sample groups (e. - **mass-spectrometry-benchmark-analysis**: Use when you have implemented or modified a tandem mass spectrometry formula inference model and need to measure whether a specific architectural change (e. - **mass-spectrometry-chromatogram-generation**: Use when after MS2 annotation and sample alignment have been completed in JPA, when you need to visualize ion chromatograms for quality control, validate feature identities, or export chromatographic evidence for specific metabolic features across multiple samples. - **mass-spectrometry-cluster-detection**: Use when you have 32-dimensional GLEAMS embeddings (output from the `gleams embed` step) and need to group spectra by their underlying peptide identity. - **mass-spectrometry-column-polarity-filtering**: Use when you have a combined EI library (from multiple sources such as NIST, RIKEN, MoNA) and access to NIST RI database files (ri.dat and USER.
